# MyLite ↔ MySQL 8.4.9 compatibility

This document tracks MyLite's first baseline compatibility target against
MySQL 8 LTS, currently MySQL 8.4.9. The baseline tables below are the focused
near-term implementation contract. The complete compatibility inventory remains
in the [detailed compatibility tables](#detailed-compatibility-tables).

## Legend

| Mark | Meaning |
| :-: | --- |
| ✅ | Supported for the exact named surface. MySQL-feature rows require MySQL 8.4.9-compatible behavior and runtime comparison tests for relevant results, errors, warnings, metadata, affected rows, side effects, and protocol details; application-baseline rows require the exact pinned CI runner and test selection to pass. |
| 🟡 | Implemented with documented compatibility gaps, reduced fidelity, or MyLite-specific behavior that is covered by tests. |
| ⚪ | Accepted at parse or API level and intentionally handled as an embedded-compatible no-op, placeholder, warning, or diagnostic. |
| ❌ | Not implemented, rejected, or not yet MySQL-runtime verified in MyLite. |

## Baseline Compatibility

Each row tracks one baseline behavior or feature. A MySQL-feature row moves out
of `❌` only when the listed surface is implemented and covered by MySQL 8.4.9
comparison tests. An application-baseline row moves out of `❌` only when the
listed pinned runner and test selection pass in CI.

Rows should be narrow enough that `✅` means the whole row is covered. When a
feature family has supported behavior plus known gaps, split the verified
surface into a green row and leave the residual feature family yellow instead
of marking the broad family green.

### Runtime, Identity, and Diagnostics

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| Selected-schema scalar readback | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified `DATABASE()` and `SCHEMA()` scalar readback returns `NULL` without a selected schema, returns the canonical selected MyLite or built-in schema after `USE`, and clears to `NULL` when the selected user schema is dropped. | [SQL schemas](docs/compatibility/sql-schemas.md), [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline current database function](docs/specs/baseline-current-database-function/specs.md), [baseline built-in schema catalog](docs/specs/baseline-builtin-schema-catalog/specs.md) |
| Dynamic current database | 🟡 | `USE` selects catalog schemas and the synthetic `information_schema`, `mysql`, `performance_schema`, and `sys` schemas; see linked docs for remaining gaps. | [SQL schemas](docs/compatibility/sql-schemas.md), [runtime session state](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |
| Database listing baseline | 🟡 | Descriptor-driven `SHOW DATABASES` / `SHOW SCHEMAS` for synthetic built-in schemas plus MyLite catalog schemas, with limited queryable `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.SCHEMATA` rows for the same schema names; see linked docs for remaining gaps. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [baseline built-in schema catalog](docs/specs/baseline-builtin-schema-catalog/specs.md), [baseline SHOW DATABASES WHERE](docs/specs/baseline-show-databases-where/specs.md) |
| Built-in schema access semantics | 🟡 | Limited metadata-only access for supported `information_schema`, `mysql`, `performance_schema`, and `sys` objects: `USE`, listing, and introspection work where the linked docs define synthetic descriptors; see linked docs for remaining gaps. | [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [mysql schema](docs/compatibility/metadata-mysql-schema.md), [sys schema](docs/compatibility/metadata-sys-schema.md), [SQL schemas](docs/compatibility/sql-schemas.md), [SQL table DDL](docs/compatibility/sql-table-ddl.md), [SQL table DML](docs/compatibility/sql-table-dml.md) |
| Built-in schema catalog baseline | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified `SHOW DATABASES` / `SHOW SCHEMAS`, selected built-in schemas, and `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.SCHEMATA` rows for MyLite's synthetic built-in schema names. | [SQL schemas](docs/compatibility/sql-schemas.md), [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [baseline built-in schema catalog](docs/specs/baseline-builtin-schema-catalog/specs.md) |
| Built-in schema table directory baseline | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified built-in table-directory counts, hashes, representative `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES`, `SHOW FULL TABLES`, and `SHOW TABLE STATUS` metadata for the four built-in schemas. | [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [baseline built-in schema table directory](docs/specs/baseline-built-in-schema-table-directory/specs.md) |
| INFORMATION_SCHEMA core/static catalog baselines | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified queryable core information-schema metadata and static catalog rows for MyLite's supported synthetic descriptor subset. | [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md), [baseline INFORMATION_SCHEMA core](docs/specs/baseline-information-schema-core/specs.md), [baseline INFORMATION_SCHEMA static catalogs](docs/specs/baseline-information-schema-static-catalogs/specs.md) |

### SQL Modes

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| MySQL default SQL modes | 🟡 | Limited scalar `@@sql_mode` reads, fixed global value, and mutable session-local `SET sql_mode` support expose the default 8.4.9 mode set and canonical assigned mode strings; only listed partial effects are implemented. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [runtime system variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-system-variables.md) |
| `NO_AUTO_VALUE_ON_ZERO` | 🟡 | Limited effect for current descriptor-owned `AUTO_INCREMENT` `INSERT` paths: explicit `0` stores zero instead of being replaced by a generated value; exact InnoDB-style counter reservation gaps after all prior insert patterns remain outside the current slice. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES` | 🟡 | Limited lexer/string-decoding effect for later admitted string literals. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `NO_ZERO_DATE` | 🟡 | Limited support for `NO_ZERO_DATE` in the documented subset; see linked docs for exact scope and gaps. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `NO_ZERO_IN_DATE` | 🟡 | Limited support for `NO_ZERO_IN_DATE` in the documented subset; see linked docs for exact scope and gaps. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode`; see linked docs for exact scope and gaps. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `PIPES_AS_CONCAT` | 🟡 | Session-mode readback plus limited string-concat behavior for documented `\|\|` contexts; see linked spec for gaps. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [baseline PIPES_AS_CONCAT](docs/specs/baseline-pipes-as-concat/specs.md) |
| `STRICT_ALL_TABLES` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode`; see linked docs for exact scope and gaps. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `STRICT_TRANS_TABLES` | 🟡 | Accepted and reflected in session `@@sql_mode` with verified MySQL warning behavior; see linked docs for exact scope and gaps. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `ANSI` | 🟡 | Accepted as a composite session mode; its `ANSI_QUOTES`, `REAL_AS_FLOAT`, and limited `PIPES_AS_CONCAT` effects are currently observable. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [baseline PIPES_AS_CONCAT](docs/specs/baseline-pipes-as-concat/specs.md) |
| `ANSI_QUOTES` | 🟡 | Limited lexer effect for later double-quoted identifiers. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| `REAL_AS_FLOAT` | 🟡 | Limited DDL effect for later `REAL` column definitions, which map to MyLite's `FLOAT` descriptor subset. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md) |
| SQL mode session-state baselines | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified default `@@sql_mode`, session assignment/readback, selected mode effects for `PIPES_AS_CONCAT` and zero-temporal handling, and unsupported-mode diagnostics for the documented subset. | [runtime session state and SQL modes](docs/compatibility/runtime-session-sql-modes.md), [runtime system variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-system-variables.md), [baseline SQL mode session state](docs/specs/baseline-sql-mode-session-state/specs.md), [baseline SQL mode system variable](docs/specs/baseline-sql-mode-system-variable/specs.md), [baseline PIPES_AS_CONCAT](docs/specs/baseline-pipes-as-concat/specs.md), [baseline zero temporal SQL modes](docs/specs/baseline-zero-temporal-sql-modes/specs.md) |

### Aggregate Functions

The parser admits common aggregate-window `OVER` syntax for the current
`COUNT(*)`, `COUNT(column)`, `COUNT(DISTINCT column)`, supported row-scalar
arguments for `SUM()` / `AVG()` / `MIN()` / `MAX()`, bitwise aggregates,
statistical aggregates, and `GROUP_CONCAT(...)`, plus JSON aggregate-window
placeholders such as `JSON_ARRAYAGG(...) OVER ...` and
`JSON_OBJECTAGG(...) OVER ...`. Execution of aggregate-window forms is explicitly
unsupported. See [parser aggregate window syntax](docs/specs/parser-aggregate-window-syntax/specs.md)
and [parser corpus JSON/statistical aggregate window surfaces](docs/specs/parser-corpus-json-stat-aggregate-window-surfaces/specs.md).

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| `ANY_VALUE()` | 🟡 | Scalar/row-scalar wrapper plus mixed ungrouped and grouped descriptor-column forms, including grouped alias `HAVING`, alias comparison `HAVING`, alias ordering, and selected/hidden expression ordering; direct grouped `HAVING ANY_VALUE(column)` operands reject with MySQL-compatible unknown-column diagnostics. Mixed/grouped expression arguments, `DISTINCT`, `*`, windows, and deterministic representative-row selection remain limited. | [aggregate functions](docs/compatibility/functions-aggregate.md), [baseline ANY_VALUE function](docs/specs/baseline-any-value-function/specs.md), [baseline mixed ungrouped ANY_VALUE aggregate](docs/specs/baseline-mixed-ungrouped-any-value-aggregate/specs.md), [baseline grouped ANY_VALUE aggregate alias order](docs/specs/baseline-grouped-any-value-aggregate-alias-order/specs.md), [baseline grouped ANY_VALUE aggregate alias HAVING](docs/specs/baseline-grouped-any-value-aggregate-alias-having/specs.md), [baseline grouped ANY_VALUE aggregate alias comparison HAVING](docs/specs/baseline-grouped-any-value-aggregate-alias-comparison-having/specs.md), [baseline grouped selected ANY_VALUE expression order](docs/specs/baseline-grouped-selected-any-value-expression-order/specs.md), [baseline hidden grouped aggregate order keys](docs/specs/baseline-hidden-grouped-aggregate-order-keys/specs.md) |

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| `BENCHMARK()` | 🟡 | MySQL-runtime-verified result shape and negative-count warning in scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts; repeated-expression timing fidelity is intentionally not implemented. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline named lock and information functions](docs/specs/baseline-named-lock-and-info-functions/specs.md) |
| `DATABASE()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified selected-schema readback in documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts; stored-routine schema binding waits for stored-program support. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline current database function](docs/specs/baseline-current-database-function/specs.md), [baseline current database row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-current-database-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md) |
| `ExtractValue()` | 🟡 | MySQL-runtime-verified simple XML/XPath baseline with MySQL-shaped malformed-XML warnings and XPath errors; full XML/XPath support remains a tracked gap. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline system function residuals](docs/specs/baseline-system-function-residuals/specs.md) |
| `SCHEMA()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified synonym for `DATABASE()` in the documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline current database function](docs/specs/baseline-current-database-function/specs.md), [baseline current database row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-current-database-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md) |
| `CONNECTION_ID()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified MyLite handle-local connection id readback in documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts, plus `DO`, `SET @user_variable`, and source-free DML value use; server thread, process-list, Performance Schema, protocol, and `pseudo_thread_id` identity semantics are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline connection ID function](docs/specs/baseline-connection-id-function/specs.md), [baseline CONNECTION_ID row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-connection-id-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md), [parser corpus SET/DML scalar values](docs/specs/parser-corpus-set-dml-scalar-value-surfaces/specs.md) |
| `CURRENT_ROLE()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified no-active-role readback in documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts; role catalog, grants, `SET ROLE`, active-role state, and bare `CURRENT_ROLE` are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [SQL users, roles, and privileges](docs/compatibility/sql-users-privileges.md), [baseline current role function](docs/specs/baseline-current-role-function/specs.md), [baseline identity row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-identity-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md) |
| `FOUND_ROWS()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified found-row readback in documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts, plus `DO`, `SET @user_variable`, source-free DML value use, and limited `SQL_CALC_FOUND_ROWS`; protocol, `CLIENT_FOUND_ROWS`, replication, and full diagnostics-area behavior are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline session diagnostics row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-session-diagnostics-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md), [parser corpus SET/DML scalar values](docs/specs/parser-corpus-set-dml-scalar-value-surfaces/specs.md) |
| `GET_LOCK()` | 🟡 | MySQL-runtime-verified recursive process-local named locks with cross-handle ownership and invalid-name diagnostics; blocking waits and Performance Schema lock rows are tracked gaps. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline named lock and information functions](docs/specs/baseline-named-lock-and-info-functions/specs.md) |
| `CURRENT_USER()` / `CURRENT_USER` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified embedded current identity readback in documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts; account and definer semantics are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline current user identity](docs/specs/baseline-current-user-identity/specs.md), [baseline identity row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-identity-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md) |
| `ICU_VERSION()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified MySQL 8.4.9 target ICU version identity string without linking ICU into MyLite. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline named lock and information functions](docs/specs/baseline-named-lock-and-info-functions/specs.md) |
| `INET_ATON()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified IPv4 conversion in scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts, `DO`, `SET @user_variable`, and source-free DML values; IPv6 helper functions are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline IP address functions](docs/specs/baseline-ip-address-functions/specs.md) |
| `INET_NTOA()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified IPv4 conversion in scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts, `DO`, `SET @user_variable`, and source-free DML values; IPv6 helper functions are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline IP address functions](docs/specs/baseline-ip-address-functions/specs.md) |
| `IS_FREE_LOCK()` | 🟡 | MySQL-runtime-verified against MyLite's process-local named-lock registry. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline named lock and information functions](docs/specs/baseline-named-lock-and-info-functions/specs.md) |
| `IS_USED_LOCK()` | 🟡 | MySQL-runtime-verified against MyLite's process-local named-lock registry with owning connection id reporting. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline named lock and information functions](docs/specs/baseline-named-lock-and-info-functions/specs.md) |
| `LAST_INSERT_ID()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified readback and supported `LAST_INSERT_ID(expr)` state-setting forms in scalar and row-scalar expression contexts, including sequence-style single-table `UPDATE`; broader conversion, protocol, mixed-mode allocation, and stored-program behavior are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline session diagnostics row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-session-diagnostics-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md), [baseline LAST_INSERT_ID expression](docs/specs/baseline-last-insert-id-expression/specs.md), [baseline LAST_INSERT_ID row expression](docs/specs/baseline-last-insert-id-row-expression/specs.md), [parser corpus SET/DML scalar values](docs/specs/parser-corpus-set-dml-scalar-value-surfaces/specs.md) |
| `LOAD_FILE()` | 🟡 | MySQL-runtime-verified embedded placeholder evaluates the argument and returns `NULL`, matching unavailable-file behavior without server-side filesystem reads. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline system function residuals](docs/specs/baseline-system-function-residuals/specs.md) |
| `NAME_CONST()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified literal values and decoded result labels in scalar and row-scalar contexts; unsupported dynamic forms return MySQL-shaped `1210` errors. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline system function residuals](docs/specs/baseline-system-function-residuals/specs.md) |
| `RELEASE_ALL_LOCKS()` | 🟡 | MySQL-runtime-verified for current-connection recursive hold release counts in MyLite's process-local named-lock registry. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline named lock and information functions](docs/specs/baseline-named-lock-and-info-functions/specs.md) |
| `RELEASE_LOCK()` | 🟡 | MySQL-runtime-verified for current-connection recursive named-lock release and unheld-lock `NULL` behavior. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline named lock and information functions](docs/specs/baseline-named-lock-and-info-functions/specs.md) |
| `ROW_COUNT()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified row-count readback in documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts, plus `DO`, `SET @user_variable`, and source-free DML value use; protocol OK-packet parity, `CLIENT_FOUND_ROWS`, and full diagnostics-area behavior are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline session diagnostics row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-session-diagnostics-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md), [parser corpus SET/DML scalar values](docs/specs/parser-corpus-set-dml-scalar-value-surfaces/specs.md) |
| `SESSION_USER()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified no-whitespace embedded client identity readback in documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts; `IGNORE_SPACE`, stored-function resolution, authentication, and host matching are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline session/system user identity](docs/specs/baseline-session-system-user-identity/specs.md), [baseline identity row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-identity-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md) |

| `SYSTEM_USER()` | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified no-whitespace embedded client identity readback in documented scalar and row-scalar SELECT contexts; `SYSTEM_USER` privilege semantics, authentication, and host matching are tracked separately. | [system functions](docs/compatibility/functions-system.md), [baseline session/system user identity](docs/specs/baseline-session-system-user-identity/specs.md), [baseline identity row-scalar contexts](docs/specs/baseline-identity-row-scalar-contexts/specs.md) |

### SQL Routines

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| `CREATE PROCEDURE` | 🟡 | Limited session-local no-argument procedure descriptors with one `SELECT` body inside `BEGIN ... END`; broader procedure syntax is parsed and rejected as unsupported; see linked docs for exact scope and gaps. | [SQL routines](docs/compatibility/sql-routines.md), [baseline stored procedure SELECT call](docs/specs/baseline-stored-procedure-select-call/specs.md), [parser admin and stored-program placeholders](docs/specs/parser-admin-stored-program-placeholders/specs.md) |
| `DROP PROCEDURE` | 🟡 | Limited session-local descriptor removal for the current no-argument single-`SELECT` procedure subset, including `IF EXISTS` note warning behavior; no persistent metadata cleanup. | [SQL routines](docs/compatibility/sql-routines.md), [baseline stored procedure SELECT call](docs/specs/baseline-stored-procedure-select-call/specs.md) |
| `CALL` | 🟡 | Limited support for `CALL` in the documented subset; see linked docs for exact scope and gaps. | [SQL routines](docs/compatibility/sql-routines.md), [baseline stored procedure SELECT call](docs/specs/baseline-stored-procedure-select-call/specs.md), [parser admin and stored-program placeholders](docs/specs/parser-admin-stored-program-placeholders/specs.md) |

### SHOW and Introspection Statements

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| `SHOW CHARACTER SET` / `SHOW CHARSET` | 🟡 | Metadata-only MySQL 8.4.9 catalog rows for all 41 character sets, with MySQL column labels plus `LIKE` and documented `WHERE` filters; catalog visibility does not imply DDL admission, conversion, comparison semantics, or privileges. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [character sets](docs/compatibility/character-sets.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |
| SHOW character set and collation catalogs | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified metadata-only `SHOW CHARACTER SET` / `SHOW CHARSET` and `SHOW COLLATION` row catalogs expose the target MySQL 8.4.9 column labels, values, warning behavior, `LIKE` filters, and documented `WHERE` filters. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [baseline SHOW CHARACTER SET and SHOW COLLATION](docs/specs/baseline-show-character-set-collation/specs.md) |
| `SHOW COLLATION` | 🟡 | Metadata-only MySQL 8.4.9 catalog rows for all 286 collations, with MySQL column labels plus `LIKE` and documented `WHERE` filters; catalog visibility does not imply DDL admission, conversion, comparison semantics, or privileges. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [collations](docs/compatibility/collations.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables](docs/compatibility/metadata-information-schema.md) |
| `SHOW COUNT(*) ERRORS` | 🟡 | Limited previous-statement error-condition count with MySQL 8.4.9 column label; counts MyLite's previous error condition only. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md) |
| `SHOW COUNT(*) WARNINGS` | 🟡 | Limited previous-statement diagnostics count with MySQL 8.4.9 column label; counts MyLite previous error conditions and stored warning/note records only, with documented missing-schema `DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS` counted-only result behavior. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md) |
| `SHOW COLUMNS` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented introspection subset; see linked spec for filters, metadata, and gaps. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [SQL table DDL](docs/compatibility/sql-table-ddl.md), [mysql schema](docs/compatibility/metadata-mysql-schema.md), [sys schema](docs/compatibility/metadata-sys-schema.md) |
| `SHOW FIELDS` | 🟡 | Limited alias for the supported `SHOW COLUMNS` subset. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md) |
| `SHOW CREATE DATABASE` / `SHOW CREATE SCHEMA` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented introspection subset; see linked spec for filters, metadata, and gaps. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [SQL schemas](docs/compatibility/sql-schemas.md) |
| `SHOW CREATE PROCEDURE` | 🟡 | Limited MySQL-shaped six-column metadata for session-local no-argument single-`SELECT` procedures created by the current routine bridge; no persistent routine catalog, privilege filtering, mutable quote-control state, or full stored-program support. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [SQL routines](docs/compatibility/sql-routines.md), [baseline stored procedure SELECT call](docs/specs/baseline-stored-procedure-select-call/specs.md) |

| SHOW STATUS core lifecycle placeholders | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified `Bytes_%`, `Connections`, session/local `Compression`, `Prepared_stmt_count`, `Queries`, `Questions`, `Threads_%`, and `Uptime%` rows expose the MySQL 8.4.9 row names and scopes with deterministic embedded placeholder values; live network, statement, thread-cache, compression, and uptime accounting remains outside this row. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [server status variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-status-variables.md), [baseline SHOW STATUS](docs/specs/baseline-show-status/specs.md), [baseline SHOW STATUS core lifecycle](docs/specs/baseline-show-status-core-lifecycle/specs.md) |
| SHOW STATUS server and legacy scalar placeholders | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified ACL, delayed-insert, flush, key-cache, last-query, max-execution/max-used-connection, replication temp-table, resource-group, secondary-engine, and telemetry scalar rows expose the MySQL 8.4.9 row names and scopes with deterministic embedded placeholder values; live server-internal accounting remains outside this row. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [server status variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-status-variables.md), [baseline SHOW STATUS](docs/specs/baseline-show-status/specs.md), [baseline SHOW STATUS server legacy scalars](docs/specs/baseline-show-status-server-legacy-scalars/specs.md) |

| SHOW STATUS connection diagnostic placeholders | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified `SHOW STATUS LIKE 'Connection\_%'` exposes the eight MySQL 8.4.9 connection-control/error diagnostic names for default/session/local/global scopes with deterministic embedded `0` values; live connection accounting remains outside this row. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [server status variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-status-variables.md), [baseline SHOW STATUS](docs/specs/baseline-show-status/specs.md), [baseline SHOW STATUS connection diagnostics](docs/specs/baseline-show-status-connection-diagnostics/specs.md) |

| SHOW STATUS temporary/open resource placeholders | ✅ | MySQL-runtime-verified `SHOW STATUS LIKE 'Created\_%'` and `SHOW STATUS LIKE 'Open%'` expose the ten MySQL 8.4.9 temporary-table and open-resource counter names for default/session/local/global scopes with deterministic embedded `0` values; live temporary table, open file, and table-cache accounting remains outside this row. | [SQL SHOW statements](docs/compatibility/sql-show-statements.md), [server status variables](docs/compatibility/runtime-status-variables.md), [baseline SHOW STATUS](docs/specs/baseline-show-status/specs.md), [baseline SHOW STATUS temporary/open resources](docs/specs/baseline-show-status-resource-counters/specs.md) |

### Transactions, Locking, and Maintenance

| Feature | Status | Notes | Full table |
| --- | :-: | --- | --- |
| `START TRANSACTION` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `BEGIN` | 🟡 | Limited alias for `START TRANSACTION`; `BEGIN WORK` is also admitted. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `BEGIN WORK` | 🟡 | Limited start-transaction synonym. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md), [baseline BEGIN IMMEDIATE compatibility](docs/specs/baseline-begin-immediate-compatibility/specs.md) |
| `COMMIT` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md), [parser corpus admin transaction surfaces](docs/specs/parser-corpus-admin-transaction-surfaces/specs.md) |
| `ROLLBACK` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md), [parser corpus admin transaction surfaces](docs/specs/parser-corpus-admin-transaction-surfaces/specs.md) |
| `SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | Limited partial rollback for current user-visible savepoints, including `ROLLBACK TO`, `ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT`, and `ROLLBACK WORK TO [SAVEPOINT]`; missing savepoints return MySQL-compatible error `1305`; no stored-program scoping or lock/protocol semantics. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `RELEASE SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | Limited release of current user-visible savepoints and later savepoints in the active explicit transaction, with MySQL-compatible missing-savepoint diagnostics; no stored-program scoping or protocol semantics. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `SET TRANSACTION` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL transactions](docs/compatibility/sql-transactions.md) |
| `LOCK TABLES` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL locking](docs/compatibility/sql-locking.md) |
| `UNLOCK TABLES` | 🟡 | Limited `UNLOCK TABLE[S]` releases the current connection-local lock intent and returns the MySQL-shaped non-row success surface; no global read lock handling or broader lock enforcement. | [SQL locking](docs/compatibility/sql-locking.md) |
| `ANALYZE TABLE` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md), [parser utility and diagnostics placeholders](docs/specs/parser-utility-diagnostics-placeholders/specs.md), [parser corpus admin and SET residuals](docs/specs/parser-corpus-admin-set-residuals/specs.md) |
| `CHECK TABLE` / `CHECK TABLES` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md), [baseline table DDL option composition](docs/specs/baseline-table-ddl-option-composition/specs.md) |
| `CHECKSUM TABLE` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md), [baseline spec](docs/specs/baseline-checksum-table/specs.md) |
| `OPTIMIZE TABLE` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md), [parser corpus admin and SET residuals](docs/specs/parser-corpus-admin-set-residuals/specs.md) |
| `REPAIR TABLE` | 🟡 | Supported for the documented embedded transaction/maintenance subset; see linked spec for semantic gaps. | [SQL table maintenance](docs/compatibility/sql-table-maintenance.md) |

## Compatibility Gap Tracking

Green rows are scoped claims, not marketing totals. A broad MySQL feature
family stays yellow when any part of the named row still has documented gaps.
To make progress visible, split a verified subset into its own green row and
leave the rest as a yellow residual row with explicit exclusions.

Application-suite rows are interpreted differently from MySQL feature rows.
They are green only for the exact pinned CI runner and test selection described
by their specs. When an application exposes a missing MySQL behavior, the
feature must still be specified, tested against MySQL 8.4.9 where applicable,
and tracked in the relevant SQL compatibility table.

## Maintenance rules

- Keep this file as an implementation contract, not a marketing scorecard.
- Split rows when syntax, behavior, metadata, warnings, or side effects need independent tests.
- Document deliberate embedded-design incompatibilities explicitly instead of silently dropping them.
- Keep this baseline tracker, `docs/compatibility/`, feature guides, design notes, and MySQL-runtime tests in sync.
- Prefer MySQL 8.4.9 behavior over convenience, SQLite defaults, or older MySQL/MariaDB behavior.
- Every implemented row needs MySQL 8.4.9 comparison tests.
- Tests must cover results, errors, warnings, metadata, affected rows, inserted ids, session state, and side effects.
- Syntax-only compatibility must still test parser acceptance and exact warning, error, or placeholder behavior.
- Add focused guide documentation when behavior needs design rationale.
- When MySQL behavior depends on platform or storage engine, tests must document the MyLite contract and observed MySQL baseline.
